This establishes accepted declarations and expressions, not runtime polling, arbitrary const evaluation, or every trait interaction.
  Missing forms: inherent, trait, trait_impl
    inherent: An associated constant defined in an inherent implementation.
    trait: An associated constant declared in a trait.
    trait_impl: An associated constant defined in a trait implementation.
